# Current finding disposition — 2026-10-20

Application revision: `87c15e1e5d89344db7dcaaa85ebb1f6972c0c829`, with retained uncommitted changes.
Full checker exit 1: **1,614 displayed failures, 1,629 individual failures and one warning**. All
873 project-model targets and all enabled checkers are unchanged from the prior target capture. The
twenty third-party models in the 893-model catalog are excluded by the existing upstream
project-model definition; no new exclusion was introduced.

This ledger retains every original stable ID, including reports absent today, and appends four newly
visible OperatorLifecycleRequest deletion-policy reports. Baseline causes, index/FK metadata, plans
and test mappings are retained in [the historical ledger](findings.md), using the same IDs. Absent
means absent from the completed current run; it does not establish production migration safety or
attribute preexisting changes to this session. No finding is suppressed.

C11/C12 update: 69 NOT NULL model findings resolved, twelve new create-context ColumnPresence
reports retained. Current identities total 2,194; historical records remain.

C13 update: twelve Vocabulary.key UniqueIndex reports resolved; see
[all 605 reviewed index identities](unique-index-review.md).

Terminal refresh: a concurrently introduced migration removed 24 PK-containing UniqueIndex and 24
RedundantUniqueIndex reports. These 48 are separate from C13's twelve validation fixes; see R7M2
evidence and the session evidence.

C14–C27 update: 118 UniqueIndex reports resolved across thirteen bounded root-cause slices; C20
trial reverted. Current UniqueIndex remainder: **451**. See U9K4 evidence and cycles.md; no checker
exclusions.

C28–C31 update: nine more exact-tuple validations; UniqueIndex **451 -> 442**. See S2V6 evidence.
Historical snapshots above retain their original counts.

C32 update: six session-limit reports resolved; current UniqueIndex **436**. All historical snapshot
